<script>
function getQueryVariable(keywords)
{
       var query = window.location.search.substring(1);
       var vars = query.split("&");
       for (var i=0;i<vars.length;i++) {
               var pair = vars[i].split("=");
               if(pair[0] == "keywords"){return pair[1];}
       }
       return(false);
}

var x = getQueryVariable();
x = x.replace(/\+/g, '%20');
x = decodeURIComponent(x);

var individual = x;
//var individual = getQueryVariable();
if (individual != false) {
 window.onload = function enterName() {
document.getElementById('edit-combine-1').value = individual;
document.getElementById("views-exposed-form-phonebook-overview-page-1").submit();
};
};
</script> </div>

jQuery(window).load(function(){
	jQuery('.slider_title').html(jQuery('.views-row-1 .hidden_title').html());
});
jQuery(document).ready(function($){
	
    var slider = $('.front_slider_container ul').bxSlider({
        mode: 'fade',
        easing: 'swing',
        useCSS : true,
        infiniteLoop: true,
        auto: true,
        pager: false,
		onSlideBefore: 	function($slide, oldIndex, newIndex){
							$('.slider_title').html($('.views-row-'+(newIndex+1)+' .hidden_title').html());
							if($(window).width() < 768){
								$('.slider_buttons .item-list .views-row').each(function(){$(this).hide()});
								$('.slider_buttons .item-list .views-row:nth-child('+(newIndex+1)+')').show();
							}
							$('.button_selected').removeClass('button_selected')
							$('.slider_buttons .item-list .views-row:nth-child('+(newIndex+1)+')').addClass('button_selected');
						} 
    });
	
	
	$('#page_buttons > a').mouseenter(function(){
		$(this).next().stop(true, true).fadeIn();
		$(this).parent().addClass('fade_in');
	});
	$('.slide-wrap').mouseleave(function(){
		var $el = $(this).find('.button_description')
		
		$el.stop(true, true).fadeOut();
		$(this).find('#page_buttons').removeClass('fade_in')
	});
	
});
</script>
